 Check,\n  Plus,\n  BookOpen,\n  Sparkles\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport { WordCard, CardFilter } from './types';\nimport { INITIAL_CARDS } from './data';\nimport FlashcardItem from './components/FlashcardItem';\n\nconst STORAGE_KEY = 'flashcards_data';\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [cards, setCards] = useState<WordCard[]>(() => {\n    try {\n      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);\n      if (saved) {\n        return JSON.parse(saved);\n      }\n    } catch {\n      // LocalStorage access failed\n    }\n    return INITIAL_CARDS;\n  });\n\n  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [isFlipped, setIsFlipped] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<CardFilter>('all');\n  const [isAddModalOpen, setIsAddModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // 単語新規追加フォームの状態\n  const [newWord, setNewWord] = useState('');\n  const [newPhonetic, setNewPhonetic] = useState('');\n  const setNewExampleJa] = useState('');\n  const [newCategory, setNewCategory] = useState('日常会話');\n\n  // cards更新時の永続化\n  useEffect(() => {\n    try {\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(cards));\n    } catch {\n      // LocalStorage save failed\n    }\n  }, [cards]);\n\n  // フィルタリングされたカード一覧\n  const filteredCards = useMemo(() => {\n    return cards.filter((card) => {\n      if (filter === 'learning') return !card.isMastered;\n      if (filter === 'mastered') return card.isMastered;\n      return true;\n    });\n  }, [cards, filter]);\n\n  // フィルタ変更時または配列縮小時にインデックス範囲を安全に保つ\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (currentIndex >= filteredCards.length) {\n      setCurrentIndex(Math.max(0, filteredCards.length - 1));\n    }\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n  }, [filter, filteredCards.length, currentIndex]);\n\n  const currentCard = filteredCards[currentIndex];\n\n  // 全体の習得度計算\n  const masteredCount = useMemo(() => cards.filter((c) => c.isMastered).length, [cards]);\n  const totalCount = cards.length;\n  const progressPercent = totalCount > 0 ? Math.round((masteredCount / totalCount) * 100) : 0;\n\n  // カードをめくる\n  const handleFlip = useCallback(() => {\n    setIsFlipped((prev) => !prev);\n  }, []);\n\n  // 次のカードへ\n  const handleNext = useCallback(() => {\n    if (currentIndex < filteredCards.length - 1) {\n      setIsFlipped(false);\n      setCurrentIndex((prev) => prev + 1);\n    }\n  }, [currentIndex, filteredCards.length]);\n\n  // 前のカードへ\n  const handlePrev = useCallback(() => {\n    if (currentIndex > 0) {\n      setIsFlipped(false);\n      setCurrentIndex((prev) => prev - 1);\n    }\n  }, [currentIndex]);\n\n  // 覚えた/復習中ステータス切り替え\n  const handleToggleMastered = useCallback(\n    (id: string) => {\n      setCards((prevCards) =>\n        prevCards.map((card) =>\n          card.id === id ? { ...card, isMastered: !card.isMastered } : card\n        )\n      );\n    },\n    []\n  );\n\n  // シャッフル\n  const handleShuffle = () => {\n    setCards((prev) => {\n      const copy = [...prev];\n      for (let i = copy.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {\n        const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));\n        [copy[i], copy[j]] = [copy[j], copy[i]];\n      }\n      return copy;\n    });\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n  };\n\n  // キーボードショートカット\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (isAddModalOpen) return;\n      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) {\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (e.code === 'Space') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleFlip();\n      } else if (e.code === 'ArrowRight') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleNext();\n      } else if (e.code === 'ArrowLeft') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handlePrev();\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n  }, [handleFlip, handleNext, handlePrev, isAddModalOpen]);\n\n  // 単語新規登録送信\n  const handleAddSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    if (!newWord.trim() || !newMeaning.trim()) return;\n\n    const newCard: WordCard = {\n      id: `card-${Date.now()}`,\n      word: newWord.trim(),\n      phonetic: newPhonetic.trim() || `/${newWord.trim().toLowerCase()}/`,\n      partOfSpeech: newPartOfSpeech,\n      meaning: newMeaning.trim(),\n      exampleEn: newExampleEn.trim() || `${newWord.trim()} is useful.`,\n      exampleJa: newExampleJa.trim() || `${newWord.trim()}は役に立ちます。`,\n      isMastered: false,\n      category: newCategory\n    };\n\n    setCards((prev) => [newCard, ...prev]);\n    setIsAddModalOpen(false);\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n\n    // フォームリセット\n    setNewWord('');\n data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/FlashcardItem.tsx","content":"import { MouseEvent } from 'react';\nimport { Volume2, RotateCw } from 'lucide-react';\nimport { WordCard } from '../types';\n\ninterface FlashcardItemProps {\n  card: WordCard;\n  isFlipped: boolean;\n  onFlip: () => void;\n}\n\nconst FlashcardItem = ({\n  card,\n  isFlipped,\n  onFlip\n}: FlashcardItemProps) => {\n  const handleSpeech = (e: MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {\n    e.stopPropagation();\n    try {\n      if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) {\n        return;\n      }\n      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();\n      const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(card.word);\n      utterance.lang = 'en-US';\n      utterance.rate = 0.85;\n      utterance.onerror = () => {\n        // 音声再生失敗時のフォールバック\n      };\n      window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);\n    } catch {\n      // 音声API呼び出し例外の安全な処理\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card-container\">\n      <div\n        className=\"flashcard-wrapper\"\n        onClick={onFlip}\n        role=\"button\"\n        tabIndex={0}\n        aria-label={`${card.word}のカードをクリックして裏返す`}\n        onKeyDown={(e) => {\n          if (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter') {\n            e.preventDefault();\n            onFlip();\n          }\n        }}\n      >\n        <div className={`flashcard ${isFlipped ? 'flashcard-flipped' : ''}`}>\n          {/* 表面 */}\n          <div className=\"card-face card-front\">\n            <div className=\"card-badge\">\n              <span className=\"card-badge-pos\">{card.partOfSpeech}</span>\n              <span className=\"card-badge-cat\">{card.category}</span>\n            </div>\n\n            <div>\n              <div className=\"card-word\">\n                <span>{card.word}</span>\n                <button\n                  type=\"button\"\n                  className=\"sound-btn\"\n                  onClick={handleSpeech}\n                  title=\"発音を聞く\"\n                  aria-label=\"発音を聞く\"\n                >\n                  <Volume2 size={20} />\n                </button>\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"card-phonetic\">{card.phonetic}</div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"card-instruction\">\n              <RotateCw size={14} />\n              <span>クリックまたはスペースキーでめくる</span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          {/* 裏面 */}\n          <div className=\"card-face card-back\">\n            <div className=\"card-badge\">\n              <span className=\"card-badge-pos\">{card.partOfSpeech}</span>\n              <span className=\"card-badge-cat\">{card.category}</span>\n            </div>\n\n            <div>\n              <div className=\"card-meaning\">{card.meaning}</div>\n              <div className=\"card-example-box\">\n                <div className=\"card-example-en\">{card.exampleEn}</div>\n                <div className=\"card-example-ja\">{card.exampleJa}</div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"card-instruction\">\n              <RotateCw size={14} />\n              <span>クリックでオモテに戻る</span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default FlashcardItem;","language":"tsx"}

FlashcardItem from './components/FlashcardItem';\n\nconst STORAGE_KEY = 'flashcards_data';\n\nconst generateCardId = (): string => {\n  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function') {\n    return crypto.randomUUID();\n  }\n  return `card-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 9)}`;\n};\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [cards, setCards] = useState<WordCard[]>(() => {\n    try {\n      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);\n      if (saved) {\n        return JSON.parse(saved);\n      }\n    } catch {\n      // LocalStorage access failed\n    }\n    return INITIAL_CARDS;\n  });\n\n  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [isFlipped, setIsFlipped] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<CardFilter>('all');\n  const [isAddModalOpen, setIsAddModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // 単語新規追加フォームの状態\n  const [newWord, setNewWord] = useState('');\n  const [newPhonetic, setNewPhonetic] = useState('');\n  const [newPartOfSpeech, setNewPartOfSpeech] = useState<PartOfSpeech>('名詞');\n  const [newMeaning, setNewMeaning] = useState('');\n  const [newExampleEn, setNewExampleEn] = useState('');\n  const [newExampleJa, setNewExampleJa] = useState('');\n  const [newCategory, setNewCategory] = useState<CardCategory>('日常会話');\n\n  // cards更新時の永続化\n  useEffect(() => {\n    try {\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(cards));\n    } catch {\n      // LocalStorage save failed\n    }\n  }, [cards]);\n\n  // フィルタリングされたカード一覧\n  const filteredCards = useMemo(() => {\n    return cards.filter((card) => {\n      if (filter === 'learning') return !card.isMastered;\n      if (filter === 'mastered') return card.isMastered;\n      return true;\n    });\n  }, [cards, filter]);\n\n  // フィルタ変更時または配列縮小時にインデックス範囲を安全に保つ\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (currentIndex >= filteredCards.length) {\n      setCurrentIndex(Math.max(0, filteredCards.length - 1));\n    }\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n  }, [filter, filteredCards.length, currentIndex]);\n\n  const currentCard = filteredCards[currentIndex];\n\n  // 全体の習得度計算\n  const Math.round((masteredCount / totalCount) * 100) : 0;\n\n  // カードをめくる\n  const handleFlip = useCallback(() => {\n    setIsFlipped((prev) => !prev);\n  }, []);\n\n  // 次のカードへ\n  const handleNext = useCallback(() => {\n    if (currentIndex < filteredCards.length - 1) {\n      setIsFlipped(false);\n      setCurrentIndex((prev) => prev + 1);\n    }\n  }, [currentIndex, filteredCards.length]);\n\n  // 前のカードへ\n  const handlePrev = useCallback(() => {\n    if (currentIndex > 0) {\n      setIsFlipped(false);\n      setCurrentIndex((prev) => prev - 1);\n    }\n  }, [currentIndex]);\n\n  // 覚えた/復習中ステータス切り替え\n  const handleToggleMastered = useCallback(\n    (id: string) => {\n      setCards((prevCards) =>\n        prevCards.map((card) =>\n          card.id === id ? { ...card, isMastered: copy.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {\n        const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));\n        [copy[i], copy[j]] = [copy[j], copy[i]];\n      }\n      return copy;\n    });\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n  };\n\n  // キーボードショートカット\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (isAddModalOpen) return;\n      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) {\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (e.code === 'Space') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleFlip();\n      } else   window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n  }, [handleFlip, handleNext, handlePrev, isAddModalOpen]);\n\n  // 単語新規登録送信\n  const handleAddSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    if (!newWord.trim() || !newMeaning.trim()) return;\n\n    const newCard: WordCard = {\n      id: };\n\n    setCards((prev) => [newCard, ...prev]);\n    setIsAddModalOpen(false);\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n\n    // フォームリセット\n    setNewWord('');\n    setNewPhonetic('');\n    setNewPartOfSpeech('名詞');\n    setNewMeaning('');\n    setNewExampleEn('');\n    setNewExampleJa('');\n    setNewCategory('日常会話');\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app\">\n      {/* ヘッダー */}\n      <header className=\"header\">\n        <h1 className=\"title\">\n          <BookOpen size={28} color=\"#4f46e5\" />\n          英単語暗記カード\n        </h1>\n        <p className=\"subtitle\">3Dフリップで効率よくボキャブラリーを身につけよう</p>\n      </header>\n\n      {/* 進捗セクション */}\n      <div className=\"progress-section\">\n        <div className=\"progress-bar-bg\">\n          <div\n            className=\"progress-bar-fill\"\n            style={{ width: `${progressPercent}%` }}\n          />\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"progress-text\">\n          <span>全体の習得進捗</span>\n          <span>\n            {masteredCount} / {totalCount} 語 習得済み ({progressPercent}%)\n          </span>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* 上部コントロール & フィルタタブ */}\n      <div className=\"controls-top\">\n        <div className=\"category-tabs\">\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={`tab-btn ${filter === 'all' ? Shuffle,\n  Check,\n  Plus,\n  BookOpen,\n  Sparkles\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport { WordCard, CardFilter, PartOfSpeech, CardCategory } from './types';\nimport { INITIAL_CARDS } from LocalStorage access failed\n    }\n    return INITIAL_CARDS;\n  });\n\n  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [isFlipped, setIsFlipped] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    try {\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(cards));\n    } catch {\n      // LocalStorage save failed\n    }\n  }, [cards]);\n\n  // フィルタリングされたカード一覧\n  const useCallback(() => {\n    if (currentIndex < filteredCards.length - 1) {\n      setIsFlipped(false);\n      setCurrentIndex((prev) => prev + 1);\n    }\n  }, [currentIndex, filteredCards.length]);\n\n  // 前のカードへ\n  const handlePrev = useCallback(() => {\n    if (currentIndex > 0) {\n      setIsFlipped(false);\n      setCurrentIndex((prev) => prev - 1);\n    }\n  }, [currentIndex]);\n\n  // 覚えた/復習中ステータス切り替え\n  const handleToggleMastered = useCallback(\n    (id: string) => {\n      setCards((prevCards) =>\n        prevCards.map((card) =>\n          card.id === id ? { ...card, isMastered: !card.isMastered } : card\n        )\n      );\n    },\n    []\n  );\n\n  // シャッフル\n  const handleShuffle = () => {\n    setCards((prev) => {\n      const copy = [...prev];\n      for (let i = setCurrentIndex(0);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n  };\n\n  // キーボードショートカット\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (isAddModalOpen) return;\n      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) {\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (e.code === 'Space') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleFlip();\n      } else 単語新規登録送信\n  const handleAddSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    if (!newWord.trim() || !newMeaning.trim()) return;\n\n    const newCard: WordCard = {\n      id: */}\n      <header className=\"header\">\n        <h1 className=\"title\">\n          <BookOpen size={28} color=\"#4f46e5\" />\n          英単語暗記カード\n        </h1>\n        <p
